- S8 N=100: 1.02, 0.98, 1.00 / 0.97, 0.97, 0.96; 100,304, 100,340, 100,320 / 100,588, 101,896, 98,588.
- S8 N=200: 1.77, 1.76, 1.79 / 1.73, 1.73, 1.77; 169,496, 170,920, 169,924 / 170,652, 170,512, 166,204.
- S8 N=400: 3.38, 3.38, 3.35 / 3.35, 3.36, 3.38; 304,324, 306,252, 308,256 / 304,972, 305,904, 305,060.
- S7: 0.46, 0.46, 0.47 / 0.46, 0.46, 0.46; 44,692, 44,704, 44,696 / 44,920, 44,924, 44,972.
- trial-folds: 0.87, 0.87, 0.87 / 0.87, 0.87, 0.88; 120,132, 118,112, 119,352 / 115,316, 115,092, 114,796.
- templates_wide: 0.22, 0.23, 0.22 / 0.23, 0.22, 0.22; 61,336, 60,636, 60,012 / 60,864, 61,204, 61,380.
- accessor-wide-emit: 1.42, 1.44, 1.45 / 1.41, 1.45, 1.44; 140,352, 140,440, 140,404 / 140,464, 140,460, 140,356.
- registration-wide-obj: 6.82, 6.92, 6.84 / 6.84, 6.82, 6.85; 264,160, 263,224, 263,888 / 263,272, 264,052, 263,276.
- churn-vector: 0.46, 0.46, 0.47 / 0.47, 0.47, 0.47; 10,612 ×3 / 10,752 ×3.

Targets:
- tf wall −5%: **not met** (0). tf's peak RSS falls 3.57%.
- S8 N=400 not slower: met (−0.6%).
- No shape slower than noise: met. churn-vector's +2.2% is one tick of
  `time`'s 10 ms resolution, as it was in WP-661.
- rw peak RSS −1%: applies only if B3 lands, and it did not.
- RSS ≤ +0.3% on every shape: S7 (+0.51%), tw (+0.94%), churn-vector
  (+1.32%) and S8 N=200 (+0.35%) are over. The cause is page-level
  layout in the binary, not the heap:
  - On an empty translation unit, RSS is 8,404–8,424 KB on main and
    8,684 KB on the branch (+270 KB). Massif's peak heap there is the
    same: 369,443 B on main against 369,438 B on the branch.
  - The branch's `.text` is 35 KB larger; `.data.rel.ro` is 2 KB and
    `.rela.dyn` 7 KB smaller.
  - Massif peak heap, main → branch (`.build/wp663-massif.log`):

    | shape | main B | branch B | Δ |
    |---|--:|--:|--:|
    | templates_wide | 59,906,137 | 59,971,667 | +0.11% |
    | S7 | 43,923,152 | 41,825,950 | −4.8% |
    | churn-vector | 1,042,084 | 1,038,562 | −0.34% |
    | trial-folds | 144,488,325 | 132,986,476 | −8.0% |

### What remains

- tf's wall is unchanged. What remains on it is the declined reads
  inside expressions the leaf walk does not take (`st.arr[1]`, 4,000
  thunks): `crates/kiln_oven/src/leaf.rs:236` (`leaf_val`) and
  `:387` (`leaf_read`).
- rw: 4,000 `fold_for_overflow` thunks
  (`crates/kiln_sema/src/oven_bridge.rs:420`) and 8,003 `ImplicitCastExpr`
  folds in the cast-other bucket.
- B3, dropping failed thunks: `crates/kiln_oven/src/compile.rs:391`
  (`compile_thunk`). Worth it only together with a way to drop a
  thunk's callees; about 1.9 MB on rw.
- A3, memoising the explicit-argument substitution:
  `crates/kiln_sema/src/templ/deduce_call.rs:702`. About 0.37% of
  S8 N=100; needs a memo that leaves the AST unchanged.
- The lookup insert path: `crates/kiln_ast/src/lookup.rs:212`
  (`insert_with_flags`).
- `TokenSource::get` (`crates/kiln_parse/src/tokens.rs:202`) went from
  33.7 to 36.3 Ir per call after A1 touched `kiln_parse/src/lib.rs`. It
  makes the same 13,564,227 calls on tf, so this is code generation
  (+35.5 M Ir on tf), not work.


## WP-665: constant-image relocation lookup

A scaling profile of the same codebase found its
first superlinear term on the shape that emits one large constant (an
array of 5,705 structs holding pointers, 10 MB of IR text): the
frontend is linear in the entry count, but the codegen step after it is
not (0.5, 4.9 and 17.4 s at 1x, 7x and 14x). The spec is
spec/landed/codegen/WP-665.md. Main is 1d88bea5e.

### Cause

`crates/kiln_codegen/src/consts.rs` lowers a constant's byte image field
by field. `Img::reloc` was `self.relocs.iter().find(..)`, a linear scan
of every relocation of the image, and it ran for every field:
`image_const` first calls `member_ptr_reloc` (which scans for every
`i64` and literal-struct field, the storage of a member pointer), and the
`IrType::Ptr` arm scans again. An image with F fields and R relocations
cost F x R. `image_union_member` filtered the whole union table per
union field the same way.

Both tables are sorted where they are produced: the Oven's freeze sorts
them (`crates/kiln_oven/src/value.rs:838`), and `ConstStore::push_aggregate`
(`crates/kiln_ast/src/consts/mod.rs`, `check_image`), through which every
aggregate image is interned, rejects relocations that are not strictly
increasing in offset and union members that are not strictly increasing
in (offset, field). `kiln_abi`'s mangler and the constant printer already
binary-search the relocations. The fix does the same: `Img::reloc` is a
`binary_search_by_key`, and `Img::unions_at` starts at a `partition_point`
and yields the members at the offset in table order, so
`image_union_member` picks the same member as before. `Img::new`
restates the invariant as `debug_assert!`s. `member_ptr_reloc`'s
early-outs are unchanged.

### The reproducer

A generated table of N structs, each two nested structs of three
`{ const char *, size }` views plus two more views: 8 pointer relocations
per element. FE is `-std=c++20 -fsyntax-only`; IR is `-std=c++20 -c
-emit-llvm -disable-llvm-passes -o /dev/null`. Median of 5 runs under
`/usr/bin/time -f '%e %M'`, seconds; RSS is IR's, KB.

| N | FE | IR main | IR branch | IR−FE main | IR−FE branch | RSS main | RSS branch |
|--:|--:|--:|--:|--:|--:|--:|--:|
| 1000 | 0.04 | 0.10 | 0.06 | 0.06 | 0.02 | 53556 | 53576 |
| 2000 | 0.07 | 0.27 | 0.11 | 0.20 | 0.04 | 54508 | 54508 |
| 4000 | 0.13 | 0.83 | 0.21 | 0.70 | 0.07 | 56820 | 56828 |
| 6000 | 0.19 | 1.71 | 0.30 | 1.51 | 0.11 | 58420 | 58944 |
| 12000 | 0.38 | 6.22 | 0.60 | 5.84 | 0.22 | 100108 | 100172 |

IR−FE is linear now: 12000 is 2.0x 6000 (3.9x on main). The 6000 RSS
difference is run-to-run spread (the FE runs of the same pair differ by
2 MB the other way); no other size moves.

### A/B

Main 1d88bea5e against this branch, alternating, 3 pairs, release
builds. Each cell is the median of 5 runs, all 3 pairs shown. Load
6 to 7. All runs exited 0. The shapes are `bench/frontend` entries with
`-std=c++26` and their mode's flags (`-S -emit-llvm -o /dev/null`, or
`-c -O2` for `registration-wide-obj`; `-freflection` for
`reflection-mangle`), and `bench/oven/churn-vector.cpp` with its
`//@ args:`.

| shape | main wall s | branch wall s | main RSS KB | branch RSS KB |
|---|--:|--:|--:|--:|
| constant-table-emit | 3.02, 2.99, 2.99 | 0.49, 0.49, 0.49 | 124532, 124780, 124544 | 125272, 124596, 124608 |
| accessor-wide-emit | 1.44, 1.42, 1.43 | 1.43, 1.43, 1.45 | 140492, 140364, 140500 | 140448, 140416, 140488 |
| virtual-lattice-emit | 0.10, 0.10, 0.10 | 0.10, 0.10, 0.10 | 27996, 26940, 28128 | 26840, 28004, 26836 |
| reflection-mangle | 0.05, 0.05, 0.05 | 0.05, 0.05, 0.05 | 21220, 21224, 21224 | 21248, 21248, 21240 |
| registration-wide-obj | 6.83, 6.77, 6.85 | 6.82, 6.79, 6.86 | 264112, 263168, 263260 | 263160, 263252, 263888 |
| churn-vector | 0.47, 0.46, 0.46 | 0.47, 0.47, 0.47 | 10728, 10728, 10728 | 10712, 10712, 10712 |

`constant-table-emit` is 6.1x faster; every control is within its
spread in wall and RSS.

### Bench

`bench/frontend/constant-table-emit.cpp` (mode `emit-llvm`) is the
reproducer's shape at 8000 elements and 64,000 relocations, generated by
token-pasting macros so the entry stays one file. Baseline recorded with
the branch: 498.5 ms, 121.3 MB (main: 3.0 s; clang 19: 0.98 s).

### Byte identity

Main and the branch, `-S -emit-llvm`, stdout and exit status compared:
- `tests/codegen` (`-O0 -std=c++26`, both targets): 912 identical, 0
  different (892 compile, 20 fail the same way without the harness's
  extra flags);
- the `expect: ir` tests: 41 identical, 0 different;
- the `expect: run` tests (conformance and stress): 751 identical, 0
  different;
- the five `bench/frontend` emit shapes and the reproducer at N=6000: 6
  identical, 0 different.

1710 identical in total, 0 different.

### Sweep

`.iter().find(`/`.position(`/`.filter(` (and `.any(`) in
`crates/kiln_codegen` and `crates/kiln_llvm`, looking for a scan of a
per-image or per-constant vector inside a per-field or per-element loop.
Only `Img::reloc` and `image_union_member` have that shape; both are
fixed. Related shapes, not fixed here:
- `crates/kiln_codegen/src/consts.rs:624` (`record_elems`): the
  initializer of a field is found with `fields.iter().position` over the
  class's fields, and a base's (`:635`) over its bases, once per field of
  every element: O(fields²) per class object, so quadratic in the width
  of a class, not in the length of a table. The same function clones the
  class's `IrRecord` (`:555`, and `image_const` at `:486`) and
  collects its fields and bases once per element: linear, but a constant
  factor per element of an array of classes.
- `crates/kiln_llvm/src/builder.rs:1190`: the duplicate-case check of
  `switch` scans the earlier cases for every case, O(cases²) per switch.
- `crates/kiln_llvm/src/module.rs:484`, `:528`, `:548`, `:551`, `:599`,
  `:715`: `func_order`/`global_order` `position` when a function or
  global is replaced or retyped, O(functions) or O(globals) per
  replacement.

### What remains

After the fix, IR−FE on the reproducer is about 18 µs per element
(0.22 s at 12000), against a 0.38 s frontend. The original
shape should now scale like its frontend; the next scaling profile run
confirms it. The sweep items above are follow-ups, none measured on the
original codebase yet.
